Finally, MARS supports real-time speed adjustment via confidence thresholding: under high request load, the serving system can increase throughput on the fly without swapping models or restarting, providing a practical latency-quality knob for deployment.

</details>


### [77] [ReDAct: Uncertainty-Aware Deferral for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07036)
*Dzianis Piatrashyn,Nikita Kotelevskii,Kirill Grishchenkov,Nikita Glazkov,Ivan Nasonov,Ilya Makarov,Timothy Baldwin,Preslav Nakov,Roman Vashurin,Maxim Panov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReDAct框架通过使用大小模型组合，在不确定性高时向大模型请求决策，仅需15%的大模型调用即可达到全大模型性能，显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在序列决策中容易产生幻觉，单个错误可能导致轨迹不可逆恶化。大模型幻觉较少但成本高昂，小模型成本低但可靠性差，需要平衡性能与成本。

Method: 提出ReDAct框架：配备大小两个LLM，默认使用小模型，当小模型预测不确定性超过校准阈值时，将决策推迟给大模型处理。

Result: 在ALFWorld和MiniGrid等文本化具身环境中评估，仅需将约15%的决策推迟给大模型，即可达到完全使用大模型的性能，同时显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: ReDAct通过智能的决策推迟机制，有效解决了LLM智能体在序列决策中性能与成本的权衡问题，为实际部署提供了经济高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recently, LLM-based agents have become increasingly popular across many applications, including complex sequential decision-making problems. However, they inherit the tendency of LLMs to hallucinate, leading to incorrect decisions. In sequential settings, even a single mistake can irreversibly degrade the trajectory, making hallucinations an even bigger problem. Although larger LLMs hallucinate less, they incur a significantly higher per-token cost. In this paper, we address this tradeoff by proposing ReDAct (Reason-Defer-Act). In ReDAct, an agent is equipped with two LLMs: a small, cheap model used by default, and a large, more reliable but expensive model. When the predictive uncertainty of the small model exceeds a calibrated threshold, the decision is deferred to the large model. We evaluate our approach in text-based embodied environments such as ALFWorld and MiniGrid and show that deferring only about 15% of decisions to the large model can match the quality of using it exclusively, while significantly reducing inference costs.

</details>


### [78] [IndoBERT-Sentiment: Context-Conditioned Sentiment Classification for Indonesian Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07057)
*Muhammad Apriandito Arya Saputra,Andry Alamsyah,Dian Puteri Ramadhani,Thomhert Suprapto Siadari,Hanif Fakhrurroja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: IndoBERT-Sentiment：首个上下文条件化的印尼语情感分析模型，通过结合主题上下文显著提升分类准确性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有印尼语情感分析模型孤立地分类文本，忽略了决定情感极性的主题上下文。许多语句的情感取决于讨论的主题，上下文无关方法会导致系统性误分类。

Method: 基于IndoBERT Large（3.35亿参数）构建上下文条件化情感分类器，同时接收主题上下文和文本作为输入。在188个主题的31,360个上下文-文本对上进行训练。

Result: 模型达到F1宏平均0.856和准确率88.1%。在与三个广泛使用的印尼语情感模型的对比评估中，比最佳基线高出35.6个F1点。能正确分类上下文无关方法系统性误判的文本。

Conclusion: 上下文条件化方法（先前用于相关性分类）可有效迁移到情感分析任务，显著提升印尼语情感分类性能，解决了上下文无关方法的局限性。

Abstract: Existing Indonesian sentiment analysis models classify text in isolation, ignoring the topical context that often determines whether a statement is positive, negative, or neutral. We introduce IndoBERT-Sentiment, a context-conditioned sentiment classifier that takes both a topical context and a text as input, producing sentiment predictions grounded in the topic being discussed. Built on IndoBERT Large (335M parameters) and trained on 31,360 context-text pairs labeled across 188 topics, the model achieves an F1 macro of 0.856 and accuracy of 88.1%. In a head-to-head evaluation against three widely used general-purpose Indonesian sentiment models on the same test set, IndoBERT-Sentiment outperforms the best baseline by 35.6 F1 points. We show that context-conditioning, previously demonstrated for relevancy classification, transfers effectively to sentiment analysis and enables the model to correctly classify texts that are systematically misclassified by context-free approaches.

</details>


### [79] [SemEval-2026 Task 3: Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (DimABSA)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07066)
*Liang-Chih Yu,Jonas Becker,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Idris Abdulmumin,Lung-Hao Lee,Ying-Lung Lin,Jin Wang,Jan Philip Wahle,Terry Ruas,Natalia Loukachevitch,Alexander Panchenko,Ilseyar Alimova,Lilian Wanzare,Nelson Odhiambo,Bela Gipp,Kai-Wei Chang,Saif M. Mohammad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SemEval-2026共享任务提出维度方面情感分析(DimABSA)和维度立场分析(DimStance)，将传统ABSA扩展到价-唤醒(VA)维度建模，并引入连续F1指标评估结构化抽取和VA回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方面情感分析(ABSA)使用分类极性标签，无法捕捉情感的细微差别。需要将ABSA从消费者评论扩展到公共议题讨论，并引入维度情感表示来更精确地建模情感和立场。

Method: 提出两个任务轨道：Track A(DimABSA)包含三个子任务(维度方面情感回归、三元组抽取、四元组抽取)；Track B(DimStance)仅包含立场目标的回归子任务。引入连续F1(cF1)指标联合评估结构化抽取和VA回归。

Result: 任务吸引了400多名参与者，收到112份最终提交和42篇系统描述论文。报告了基线结果，分析了最佳性能系统，并讨论了维度情感分析在方面和立场目标层面的关键设计选择。

Conclusion: 该共享任务成功将维度情感分析扩展到方面和立场目标层面，为情感分析领域提供了新的评估框架和资源，促进了维度情感表示在结构化情感分析任务中的应用。

Abstract: We present the SemEval-2026 shared task on Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis (DimABSA), which improves traditional ABSA by modeling sentiment along valence-arousal (VA) dimensions rather than using categorical polarity labels. To extend ABSA beyond consumer reviews to public-issue discourse (e.g., political, energy, and climate issues), we introduce an additional task, Dimensional Stance Analysis (DimStance), which treats stance targets as aspects and reformulates stance detection as regression in the VA space. The task consists of two tracks: Track A (DimABSA) and Track B (DimStance). Track A includes three subtasks: (1) dimensional aspect sentiment regression, (2) dimensional aspect sentiment triplet extraction, and (3) dimensional aspect sentiment quadruplet extraction, while Track B includes only the regression subtask for stance targets. We also introduce a continuous F1 (cF1) metric to jointly evaluate structured extraction and VA regression. The task attracted more than 400 participants, resulting in 112 final submissions and 42 system description papers. We report baseline results, discuss top-performing systems, and analyze key design choices to provide insights into dimensional sentiment analysis at the aspect and stance-target levels. All resources are available on our GitHub repository.

</details>


### [80] [Is Cross-Lingual Transfer in Bilingual Models Human-Like? A Study with Overlapping Word Forms in Dutch and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07067)
*Iza Škrjanec,Irene Elisabeth Winther,Vera Demberg,Stefan L. Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 双语语言模型在词汇共享条件下能捕捉跨语言激活效应，但与人类处理的匹配程度取决于词汇重叠的编码方式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究双语语言模型是否能像人类双语者一样在阅读中表现出跨语言激活模式，特别是对同源词（朋友）和同形异义词（假朋友）的不同反应

Method: 训练荷兰语-英语因果Transformer模型，设置四种词汇共享条件（控制同源词和同形异义词是否共享嵌入），使用双语阅读研究的心理语言学刺激材料，通过惊奇度和嵌入相似性分析评估模型

Result: 模型主要保持语言分离，跨语言效应主要在嵌入共享时出现；此时同源词和同形异义词都相对于控制词表现出促进效应；回归分析显示这些效应主要由频率而非形式-意义映射的一致性驱动；只有当同源词共享嵌入时，模型才能复现人类双语者的定性模式

Conclusion: 双语语言模型能捕捉一些跨语言激活效应，但与人类处理的对齐程度关键取决于词汇重叠的编码方式，这可能限制了它们作为双语阅读模型的解释充分性

Abstract: Bilingual speakers show cross-lingual activation during reading, especially for words with shared surface form. Cognates (friends) typically lead to facilitation, whereas interlingual homographs (false friends) cause interference or no effect. We examine whether cross-lingual activation in bilingual language models mirrors these patterns. We train Dutch-English causal Transformers under four vocabulary-sharing conditions that manipulate whether (false) friends receive shared or language-specific embeddings. Using psycholinguistic stimuli from bilingual reading studies, we evaluate the models through surprisal and embedding similarity analyses. The models largely maintain language separation, and cross-lingual effects arise primarily when embeddings are shared. In these cases, both friends and false friends show facilitation relative to controls. Regression analyses reveal that these effects are mainly driven by frequency rather than consistency in form-meaning mapping. Only when just friends share embeddings are the qualitative patterns of bilinguals reproduced. Overall, bilingual language models capture some cross-linguistic activation effects. However, their alignment with human processing seems to critically depend on how lexical overlap is encoded, possibly limiting their explanatory adequacy as models of bilingual reading.

</details>


### [81] [Multilingual Embedding Probes Fail to Generalize Across Learner Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07095)
*Laurits Lyngbaek,Ross Deans Kristensen-McLachlan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多语言嵌入模型未能编码语言通用的熟练度表征：在分布内评估中探针表现良好，但在跨语料库评估中性能崩溃，表明学习到的是语料库特定分布特征而非抽象可迁移的熟练度维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多语言嵌入模型是否编码了语言通用的熟练度表征，这对于基于表征的熟练度自适应语言技术具有重要意义。

Method: 在Qwen3-Embedding（0.6B、4B、8B）的隐藏状态激活上训练线性和非线性探针，预测来自9个语料库、7种语言的学习者文本的CEFR熟练度水平，比较5种探针架构与基于表层文本特征的基线。

Result: 分布内评估中探针表现强劲（QWK≈0.7），显著优于表层基线，中间层预测效果最佳；但跨语料库评估中所有探针类型和模型大小的性能均崩溃，残差分析显示OOD探针趋于预测均匀分布标签。

Conclusion: 当前多语言嵌入模型并未直接编码语言通用的熟练度表征，学习到的映射捕获的是语料库特定分布属性（主题、语言、任务类型、评分方法）而非抽象可迁移的熟练度维度，这对基于表征的熟练度自适应语言技术具有重要启示。

Abstract: Do multilingual embedding models encode a language-general representation of proficiency? We investigate this by training linear and non-linear probes on hidden-state activations from Qwen3-Embedding (0.6B, 4B, 8B) to predict CEFR proficiency levels from learner texts across nine corpora and seven languages. We compare five probing architectures against a baseline trained on surface-level text features. Under in-distribution evaluation, probes achieve strong performance ($QWK\approx0.7$), substantially outperforming the surface baseline, with middle layers consistently yielding the best predictions. However, in cross-corpus evaluation performance collapses across all probe types and model sizes. Residual analysis reveals that out-of-distribution probes converge towards predicting uniformly distributed labels, indicating that the learned mappings capture corpus-specific distributional properties (topic, language, task type, rating methodology) rather than an abstract, transferable proficiency dimension. These results suggest that current multilingual embeddings do not straightforwardly encode language-general proficiency, with implications for representation-based approaches to proficiency-adaptive language technology.

</details>


### [82] [STRIDE-ED: A Strategy-Grounded Stepwise Reasoning Framework for Empathetic Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07100)
*Hongru Ji,Yuyin Fan,Meng Zhao,Xianghua Li,Lianwei Wu,Chao Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STRIDE-ED是一个基于策略、可解释的深度推理框架，通过结构化、策略条件化的推理来建模共情对话，解决了现有方法在共情策略框架、多阶段推理和高质量数据方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共情对话方法存在三个根本性限制：缺乏全面的共情策略框架、缺乏明确的任务对齐多阶段推理、缺乏高质量的策略感知数据，这阻碍了将共情对话有效建模为复杂的多阶段认知和决策过程。

Method: 1. 提出STRIDE-ED框架：基于策略、可解释的深度推理框架，通过结构化、策略条件化的推理建模共情对话；2. 开发策略感知数据精炼流程：集成基于LLM的标注、多模型一致性加权评估和动态采样，构建与共情策略对齐的高质量训练数据；3. 采用两阶段训练范式：结合监督微调和多目标强化学习，使模型行为更好地与目标情感、共情策略和响应格式对齐。

Result: 广泛实验表明，STRIDE-ED能够泛化到多种开源LLM，并在自动指标和人工评估上持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: STRIDE-ED通过结构化策略条件推理、高质量数据构建和两阶段训练，有效解决了共情对话建模中的关键挑战，为共情对话系统提供了更全面的解决方案。

Abstract: Empathetic dialogue requires not only recognizing a user's emotional state but also making strategy-aware, context-sensitive decisions throughout response generation. However, the lack of a comprehensive empathy strategy framework, explicit task-aligned multi-stage reasoning, and high-quality strategy-aware data fundamentally limits existing approaches, preventing them from effectively modeling empathetic dialogue as a complex, multi-stage cognitive and decision-making process. To address these challenges, we propose STRIDE-ED, a STRategy-grounded, Interpretable, and DEep reasoning framework that models Empathetic Dialogue through structured, strategy-conditioned reasoning. To support effective learning, we develop a strategy-aware data refinement pipeline integrating LLM-based annotation, multi-model consistency-weighted evaluation, and dynamic sampling to construct high-quality training data aligned with empathetic strategies. Furthermore, we adopt a two-stage training paradigm that combines supervised fine-tuning with multi-objective reinforcement learning to better align model behaviors with target emotions, empathetic strategies, and response formats. Extensive experiments demonstrate that STRIDE-ED generalizes across diverse open-source LLMs and consistently outperforms existing methods on both automatic metrics and human evaluations.

</details>


### [83] [The Impact of Steering Large Language Models with Persona Vectors in Educational Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07102)
*Yongchao Wu,Aron Henriksson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究激活导向的人格向量在短答案生成和自动评分中的教育应用效果，发现人格导向会降低答案质量，且对开放性英语语言艺术任务的影响远大于事实性科学任务；评分方面存在可预测的校准偏移，不同人格的评分者表现出系统性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然激活导向可以在推理时个性化大型语言模型，但其在教育环境中的效果尚不明确。本研究旨在系统探究人格特征导向在生成和评分任务中的影响，为教育场景中部署导向模型提供实证依据。

Method: 使用ASAP-SAS基准测试，针对三种模型（涵盖两种架构）研究七种人格特征的导向向量。在短答案生成和自动评分两个维度进行分析，比较英语语言艺术（ELA）和科学任务对人格导向的敏感性差异。

Result: 人格导向总体上降低了答案质量，对开放性ELA任务的影响比对事实性科学任务大得多（解释性和论证性任务敏感性高达11倍）。评分方面观察到可预测的校准偏移：邪恶和不礼貌的评分者更严厉，善良和乐观的评分者更宽容。ELA任务对评分者个性化的敏感性是科学任务的2.5-3倍，混合专家模型的校准偏移比密集模型大约6倍。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统研究激活导向人格特征在教育生成和评分中影响的研究。结果表明，在教育环境中部署导向模型时，需要考虑任务类型和架构差异进行校准，强调了任务感知和架构感知校准的必要性。

Abstract: Activation-based steering can personalize large language models at inference time, but its effects in educational settings remain unclear. We study persona vectors for seven character traits in short-answer generation and automated scoring on the ASAP-SAS benchmark across three models spanning two architectures. Persona steering lowers answer quality overall, with much larger effects on open-ended English Language Arts (ELA) prompts than on factual science prompts; interpretive and argumentative tasks are up to 11x more sensitive. On the scoring side, we observe predictable valence-aligned calibration shifts: evil and impolite scorers grade more harshly, while good and optimistic scorers grade more leniently. ELA tasks are 2.5-3x more susceptible to scorer personalization than science tasks, and the Mixture-of-Experts model shows roughly 6x larger calibration shifts than the dense models. To our knowledge, this is the first study to systematically examine the effects of activation-steered persona traits in educational generation and scoring, and the results highlight the need for task-aware and architecture-aware calibration when deploying steered models in educational settings.

</details>


### [84] [Yale-DM-Lab at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Deterministic Grounding and Multi-Pass Evidence Alignment for EHR Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07116)
*Elyas Irankhah,Samah Fodeh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Yale-DM-Lab系统在ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务中采用多模型集成方法处理患者对住院记录的提问，包含四个子任务：临床问题重述、证据句识别、答案生成和证据-答案对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在开发一个系统来处理患者对住院记录提出的问题，将患者语言转化为临床可解释的问题，并基于医疗记录提供准确答案和证据对齐，以改善医患沟通和医疗决策支持。

Method: 采用多模型集成策略：ST1使用Claude Sonnet 4和GPT-4o的双模型管道进行问题重述；ST2-ST4使用Azure托管的模型集成（o3、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.1、DeepSeek-R1），结合少样本提示和投票策略；ST4额外使用完整临床答案段落作为提示上下文。

Result: 在开发集上获得最佳分数：ST4达到88.81微F1，ST2达到65.72宏F1，ST3为34.01，ST1为33.05。主要发现：模型多样性和集成投票持续优于单模型基线；完整临床答案段落有助于证据对齐；对齐准确性主要受推理能力限制。

Conclusion: 多模型集成方法在医疗问答任务中表现良好，模型多样性是关键成功因素。证据-答案对齐的准确性受限于模型的推理能力，未来需要改进推理机制以提升对齐性能。

Abstract: We describe the Yale-DM-Lab system for the ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task. The task studies patient-authored questions about hospitalization records and contains four subtasks (ST): clinician-interpreted question reformulation, evidence sentence identification, answer generation, and evidence-answer alignment. ST1 uses a dual-model pipeline with Claude Sonnet 4 and GPT-4o to reformulate patient questions into clinician-interpreted questions. ST2-ST4 rely on Azure-hosted model ensembles (o3, GPT-5.2, GPT-5.1, and DeepSeek-R1) combined with few-shot prompting and voting strategies. Our experiments show three main findings. First, model diversity and ensemble voting consistently improve performance compared to single-model baselines. Second, the full clinician answer paragraph is provided as additional prompt context for evidence alignment. Third, results on the development set show that alignment accuracy is mainly limited by reasoning. The best scores on the development set reach 88.81 micro F1 on ST4, 65.72 macro F1 on ST2, 34.01 on ST3, and 33.05 on ST1.

</details>


### [85] [Are Non-English Papers Reviewed Fairly? Language-of-Study Bias in NLP Peer Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07119)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Abdulfattah Safa,Verena Blaschke,Erika Lombart,Marie-Catherine de Marneffe,Gözde Gül Şahin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次系统性地研究NLP同行评审中的语言研究偏见，区分负面和正面偏见形式，创建LOBSTER数据集，开发检测方法，分析15,645条评审，发现非英语论文面临更高偏见率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同行评审在NLP发表过程中起核心作用，但存在各种偏见。语言研究偏见（LoS bias）指评审者基于论文研究的语言而非科学价值进行不同评价的倾向。尽管评审指南明确提及，但此类偏见理解不足。先前研究将其归为宽泛的弱评审或无建设性评审类别，未将其定义为独特的偏见形式

Method: 1. 首次系统性地描述语言研究偏见，区分负面和正面形式；2. 创建人类标注的数据集LOBSTER（Language-Of-study Bias in ScienTific pEer Review）；3. 开发检测方法，达到87.37宏平均F1分数；4. 分析15,645条评审，估计负面和正面偏见如何随研究语言变化；5. 识别负面偏见的四个子类别

Result: 1. 非英语论文面临显著高于英语论文的偏见率；2. 负面偏见始终超过正面偏见；3. 识别出四个负面偏见子类别，其中"要求不合理的跨语言泛化"是最主要形式；4. 检测方法达到87.37宏平均F1分数

Conclusion: 这是首次对NLP同行评审中语言研究偏见的系统性研究，提供了数据集、检测方法和分析框架。研究发现非英语论文面临更高偏见率，负面偏见占主导地位。公开所有资源以支持NLP及其他领域更公平的评审实践

Abstract: Peer review plays a central role in the NLP publication process, but is susceptible to various biases. Here, we study language-of-study (LoS) bias: the tendency for reviewers to evaluate a paper differently based on the language(s) it studies, rather than its scientific merit. Despite being explicitly flagged in reviewing guidelines, such biases are poorly understood. Prior work treats such comments as part of broader categories of weak or unconstructive reviews without defining them as a distinct form of bias. We present the first systematic characterization of LoS bias, distinguishing negative and positive forms, and introduce the human-annotated dataset LOBSTER (Language-Of-study Bias in ScienTific pEer Review) and a method achieving 87.37 macro F1 for detection. We analyze 15,645 reviews to estimate how negative and positive biases differ with respect to the LoS, and find that non-English papers face substantially higher bias rates than English-only ones, with negative bias consistently outweighing positive bias. Finally, we identify four subcategories of negative bias, and find that demanding unjustified cross-lingual generalization is the most dominant form. We publicly release all resources to support work on fairer reviewing practices in NLP and beyond.

</details>


### [86] [Language Bias under Conflicting Information in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07123)
*Robert Östling,Murathan Kurfalı*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现多语言大语言模型在处理多语言冲突信息时存在系统性语言偏好偏见，所有测试模型都倾向于忽略冲突并自信地只给出一个答案，且对俄语有普遍偏见，在长上下文场景下对中文有偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探究大语言模型在处理多语言冲突信息时是否存在语言偏见。虽然已有研究显示LLMs在整合冲突信息时存在偏见，但尚未有研究专门探讨这些偏见是否与信息使用的语言相关。

Method: 研究方法是将"干草堆中的冲突针"范式扩展到多语言环境，使用五种不同语言的新闻领域自然数据，对一系列不同规模的多语言LLMs进行全面评估，包括GPT-5.2等模型。

Result: 研究发现所有测试的LLMs在大多数情况下都忽略冲突并自信地只给出一个可能答案。存在一致的语言偏好偏见：普遍对俄语有偏见，在最长上下文长度下对中文有偏好。这些模式在中国境内和境外训练的模型中均存在，但在前者中更为明显。

Conclusion: 结论是多语言大语言模型在处理多语言冲突信息时确实存在系统性语言偏见，这种偏见在不同模型和训练环境中具有一致性，表明需要进一步研究并开发减轻此类偏见的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been shown to contain biases in the process of integrating conflicting information when answering questions. Here we ask whether such biases also exist with respect to which language is used for each conflicting piece of information. To answer this question, we extend the conflicting needles in a haystack paradigm to a multilingual setting and perform a comprehensive set of evaluations with naturalistic news domain data in five different languages, for a range of multilingual LLMs of different sizes. We find that all LLMs tested, including GPT-5.2, ignore the conflict and confidently assert only one of the possible answers in the large majority of cases. Furthermore, there is a consistent bias across models in which languages are preferred, with a general bias against Russian and, for the longest context lengths, in favor of Chinese. Both of these patterns are consistent between models trained inside and outside of mainland China, though somewhat stronger in the former category.

</details>


### [87] [Dynamic Context Evolution for Scalable Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07147)
*Ryan Lingo,Rajeev Chhajer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE)框架，通过三种机制解决大语言模型在跨批次独立提示时出现的输出重复问题（跨批次模式坍塌），相比朴素提示显著提升输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在跨批次独立提示时会产生重复输出（跨批次模式坍塌），现有方法多为临时性的去重和种子轮换，缺乏系统性的解决方案。

Method: 提出Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE)框架，包含三个核心机制：1) 言语化尾部采样（模型标注每个想法的明显程度并丢弃明显想法）；2) 语义记忆（维护持久嵌入索引以拒绝跨批次近似重复）；3) 自适应提示演化（基于记忆状态和轮换多样性策略重构生成提示）。

Result: 在三个领域（可持续包装概念、教育考试题目、创意写作提示）和两个模型家族（gpt-5-mini和claude-haiku-4-5）的实验中，DCE实现0.0±0.0%坍塌率（朴素提示为5.6±2.0%），每种子产生17-18个HDBSCAN聚类（朴素提示为不稳定的2-17个），表明概念结构更丰富。结果经独立嵌入模型验证，对参数变化鲁棒。

Conclusion: DCE提供了一种无需微调或定制架构的轻量级解决方案，仅需标准API调用（约0.50美元/千候选），去重和提示演化单独使用不足但联合有效，能可靠缓解跨批次模式坍塌问题。

Abstract: Large language models produce repetitive output when prompted independently across many batches, a phenomenon we term cross-batch mode collapse: the progressive loss of output diversity when a language model is prompted repeatedly without access to its prior generations. Practitioners have long mitigated this with ad hoc deduplication and seed rotation, but no principled framework exists. We introduce Dynamic Context Evolution (DCE), comprising three mechanisms: (1) verbalized tail sampling (the model labels each idea with a guess about how obvious it is, and obvious ideas are discarded), which filters high-probability candidates via model self-assessment; (2) semantic memory, which maintains a persistent embedding index to reject near-duplicates across batches; and (3) adaptive prompt evolution, which reconstructs the generation prompt each batch using memory state and rotating diversity strategies. In experiments across three domains (sustainable packaging concepts, educational exam questions, and creative writing prompts) and two model families (gpt-5-mini and claude-haiku-4-5), a component ablation across 2-3 random seeds per method shows that DCE achieves 0.0 +/- 0.0% collapse versus 5.6 +/- 2.0% for naive prompting, while producing 17-18 HDBSCAN clusters per seed versus naive's volatile 2-17, indicating reliably richer conceptual structure. These results are validated with an independent embedding model (all-MiniLM-L6-v2) and hold across sensitivity sweeps of the VTS threshold tau and dedup threshold delta. Deduplication and prompt evolution are individually insufficient but jointly effective, at approximately $0.50 per 1,000 candidates using only standard API calls, with no fine-tuning or custom architectures required.

</details>


### [88] [LaScA: Language-Conditioned Scalable Modelling of Affective Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07193)
*Kosmas Pinitas,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种使用语言模型作为语义上下文调节器的新框架，通过手工制作的情感描述符来建模效价和唤醒度的变化，在保持特征可解释性的同时提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度神经嵌入的方法在无约束环境中预测情感时缺乏可解释性，限制了专家驱动的改进。需要一种既能保持特征透明度又能利用语言模型上下文抽象能力的方法。

Method: 从结构化领域知识中提取可解释的面部几何和声学特征，将其转化为编码情感含义的符号化自然语言描述，使用预训练语言模型处理这些描述生成语义上下文嵌入，作为情感动态的高层先验。

Result: 在Aff-Wild2和SEWA数据集上进行情感变化预测实验，结果显示该方法在效价和唤醒度预测准确性上均优于纯手工特征和深度嵌入基线方法。

Conclusion: 语义条件调节能够实现可解释的情感建模而不牺牲预测性能，为完全端到端架构提供了透明且计算高效的替代方案。

Abstract: Predicting affect in unconstrained environments remains a fundamental challenge in human-centered AI. While deep neural embeddings dominate contemporary approaches, they often lack interpretability and limit expert-driven refinement. We propose a novel framework that uses Language Models (LMs) as semantic context conditioners over handcrafted affect descriptors to model changes in Valence and Arousal. Our approach begins with interpretable facial geometry and acoustic features derived from structured domain knowledge. These features are transformed into symbolic natural-language descriptions encoding their affective implications. A pretrained LM processes these descriptions to generate semantic context embeddings that act as high-level priors over affective dynamics. Unlike end-to-end black-box pipelines, our framework preserves feature transparency while leveraging the contextual abstraction capabilities of LMs. We evaluate the proposed method on the Aff-Wild2 and SEWA datasets for affect change prediction. Experimental results show consistent improvements in accuracy for both Valence and Arousal compared to handcrafted-only and deep-embedding baselines. Our findings demonstrate that semantic conditioning enables interpretable affect modelling without sacrificing predictive performance, offering a transparent and computationally efficient alternative to fully end-to-end architectures

</details>


### [89] [Efficient Learned Data Compression via Dual-Stream Feature Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07239)
*Huidong Ma,Xinyan Shi,Hui Sun,Xiaofei Yue,Xiaoguang Liu,Gang Wang,Wentong Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双流多尺度解耦器，将局部和全局上下文解耦，用浅层并行流替代深层串行处理，结合分层门控细化器进行自适应特征优化，设计并发流并行流水线实现全流水线并行化，在压缩比和吞吐量上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习型数据压缩方法在精确概率建模和系统效率间难以平衡，单一流架构无法同时捕获微观句法和宏观语义特征，需要深层串行堆叠导致延迟增加，且异构系统受限于设备速度不匹配，串行处理受Amdahl定律限制吞吐量。

Method: 提出双流多尺度解耦器，将局部和全局上下文解耦，用浅层并行流替代深层串行处理；引入分层门控细化器进行自适应特征优化和精确概率建模；设计并发流并行流水线，克服系统瓶颈实现全流水线并行化。

Result: 实验表明该方法在压缩比和吞吐量上均达到最先进性能，同时保持最低延迟和内存使用，代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 通过解耦局部和全局上下文、采用并行处理架构和自适应特征优化，成功解决了学习型数据压缩中平衡精确概率建模与系统效率的挑战，实现了高性能、低延迟的压缩系统。

Abstract: While Learned Data Compression (LDC) has achieved superior compression ratios, balancing precise probability modeling with system efficiency remains challenging. Crucially, uniform single-stream architectures struggle to simultaneously capture micro-syntactic and macro-semantic features, necessitating deep serial stacking that exacerbates latency. Compounding this, heterogeneous systems are constrained by device speed mismatches, where throughput is capped by Amdahl's Law due to serial processing. To this end, we propose a Dual-Stream Multi-Scale Decoupler that disentangles local and global contexts to replace deep serial processing with shallow parallel streams, and incorporate a Hierarchical Gated Refiner for adaptive feature refinement and precise probability modeling. Furthermore, we design a Concurrent Stream-Parallel Pipeline, which overcomes systemic bottlenecks to achieve full-pipeline parallelism. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both compression ratio and throughput, while maintaining the lowest latency and memory usage. The code is available at https://github.com/huidong-ma/FADE.

</details>


### [90] [ClickGuard: A Trustworthy Adaptive Fusion Framework for Clickbait Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07272)
*Chhavi Dhiman,Naman Chawla,Riya Dhami,Gaurav Kumar,Ganesh Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ClickGuard：基于BERT嵌入和结构特征的点击诱饵检测框架，采用句法-语义自适应融合模块和混合CNN-BiLSTM架构，达到96.93%测试准确率，并通过可解释性分析验证其可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点击诱饵标题的广泛使用对在线可信度构成重大挑战，这些标题采用耸人听闻、误导性声明和模糊语言，需要有效的检测方法来确保数字内容的可信度。

Method: 提出ClickGuard框架，结合BERT嵌入和结构特征，使用句法-语义自适应融合模块（SSAFB）进行动态集成，并采用混合CNN-BiLSTM架构捕获模式特征和依赖关系。

Result: 模型达到96.93%的测试准确率，优于现有最先进方法。通过LIME和排列特征重要性（PFI）进行可解释性分析，评估模型的鲁棒性和对特征变化的敏感性。消融研究验证了SSAFB在优化特征融合方面的有效性。

Conclusion: ClickGuard框架在多个数据集上表现出稳健性能，通过解决句法-语义建模挑战，为增强在线内容可信度提供了可扩展、可靠的解决方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: The widespread use of clickbait headlines, crafted to mislead and maximize engagement, poses a significant challenge to online credibility. These headlines employ sensationalism, misleading claims, and vague language, underscoring the need for effective detection to ensure trustworthy digital content. The paper introduces, ClickGuard: a trustworthy adaptive fusion framework for clickbait detection. It combines BERT embeddings and structural features using a Syntactic-Semantic Adaptive Fusion Block (SSAFB) for dynamic integration. The framework incorporates a hybrid CNN-BiLSTM to capture patterns and dependencies. The model achieved 96.93% testing accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. The model's trustworthiness is evaluated using LIME and Permutation Feature Importance (PFI) for interpretability and perturbation analysis. These methods assess the model's robustness and sensitivity to feature changes by measuring the average prediction variation. Ablation studies validated the SSAFB's effectiveness in optimizing feature fusion. The model demonstrated robust performance across diverse datasets, providing a scalable, reliable solution for enhancing online content credibility by addressing syntactic-semantic modelling challenges. Code of the work is available at: https://github.com/palindromeRice/ClickBait_Detection_Architecture

</details>


### [91] [Evaluating In-Context Translation with Synchronous Context-Free Grammar Transduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07320)
*Jackson Petty,Jaulie Goe,Tal Linzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs在基于上下文形式语法的字符串转导任务中表现有限，准确率随语法规模和句子长度下降，形态和书写差异显著影响性能，主要错误类型包括词汇误选、幻觉和未翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLMs在低资源语言翻译中的局限性，特别是它们能否通过上下文提供的语言描述（如教科书、词典）来推断语法规则与句子之间的关系，从而减少对大量训练数据的依赖。

Method: 构建同步上下文无关文法来定义形式语言对，模拟自然语言的语法、形态和书写特征。在给定文法和源语言句子的情况下，测量LLMs将句子从一个形式语言翻译到另一个的能力。系统变化文法规模、句子长度、语言形态特征和书写系统。

Result: 1. LLMs翻译准确率随文法规模和句子长度显著下降；2. 源语言和目标语言之间的形态和书写差异会严重降低模型性能；3. 主要错误类型包括：错误回忆目标语言词汇、幻觉生成新词汇、以及未翻译源语言词汇。

Conclusion: LLMs在基于上下文语法描述的翻译任务中能力有限，特别是在处理复杂语法结构、长句和跨语言形态差异时表现不佳。这表明仅依赖上下文语言描述可能不足以解决低资源语言翻译问题。

Abstract: Low-resource languages pose a challenge for machine translation with large language models (LLMs), which require large amounts of training data. One potential way to circumvent this data dependence is to rely on LLMs' ability to use in-context descriptions of languages, like textbooks and dictionaries. To do so, LLMs must be able to infer the link between the languages' grammatical descriptions and the sentences in question. Here we isolate this skill using a formal analogue of the task: string transduction based on a formal grammar provided in-context. We construct synchronous context-free grammars which define pairs of formal languages designed to model particular aspects of natural language grammar, morphology, and written representation. Using these grammars, we measure how well LLMs can translate sentences from one formal language into another when given both the grammar and the source-language sentence. We vary the size of the grammar, the lengths of the sentences, the syntactic and morphological properties of the languages, and their written script. We note three key findings. First, LLMs' translation accuracy decreases markedly as a function of grammar size and sentence length. Second, differences in morphology and written representation between the source and target languages can strongly diminish model performance. Third, we examine the types of errors committed by models and find they are most prone to recall the wrong words from the target language vocabulary, hallucinate new words, or leave source-language words untranslated.

</details>


### [92] [Personalized RewardBench: Evaluating Reward Models with Human Aligned Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07343)
*Qiyao Ma,Dechen Gao,Rui Cai,Boqi Zhao,Hanchu Zhou,Junshan Zhang,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了Personalized RewardBench，这是一个专门评估奖励模型在个性化偏好建模能力的新基准，现有SOTA奖励模型在该基准上表现不佳（最高仅75.94%准确率），且该基准与下游任务性能相关性更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有奖励模型评估主要关注通用响应质量，缺乏对个性化用户偏好建模能力的评估。随着多元对齐成为LLM发展的关键前沿，需要专门基准来评估奖励模型如何捕捉多样化的人类价值观和个体偏好。

Method: 构建基于用户特定准则的选定和拒绝响应对，确保偏好区分完全个性化。通过人工评估确认配对的主要区分因素是个人偏好，同时保持两个响应在通用质量（正确性、相关性和帮助性）方面的高水平。进行广泛测试并与下游任务（Best-of-N采样和PPO）性能相关性分析。

Result: 现有最先进的奖励模型在个性化偏好建模上表现显著不足，最高准确率仅75.94%。Personalized RewardBench与下游任务性能的相关性显著高于现有基线，在BoN采样和PPO中都表现出更强的预测能力。

Conclusion: Personalized RewardBench是一个稳健且准确的评估基准，能够有效预测奖励模型在下游应用中的性能表现，填补了奖励模型个性化偏好评估的空白。

Abstract: Pluralistic alignment has emerged as a critical frontier in the development of Large Language Models (LLMs), with reward models (RMs) serving as a central mechanism for capturing diverse human values. While benchmarks for general response quality are prevalent, evaluating how well reward models account for individual user preferences remains an open challenge. To bridge this gap, we introduce Personalized RewardBench, a novel benchmark designed to rigorously assess reward models' capacity to model personalized preferences. We construct chosen and rejected response pairs based on strict adherence to (or violation of) user-specific rubrics, ensuring that preference distinctions are uniquely tailored to the individual. In particular, human evaluations confirm that the primary discriminative factor between pairs is strictly personal preference, with both responses maintaining high general quality (e.g., correctness, relevance and helpfulness). Extensive testing reveals that existing state-of-the-art reward models struggle significantly with personalization, peaking at an accuracy of just 75.94%. Crucially, because an effective reward model benchmark should predict a reward model's performance on downstream tasks, we conduct experiments demonstrating that our benchmark exhibits a significantly higher correlation with downstream performance in both Best-of-N (BoN) sampling and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) compared to existing baselines. These findings establish Personalized RewardBench as a robust and accurate proxy for evaluating reward models' performance in downstream applications.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [Adaptive Control with Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06338)
*Trivikram Satharasi,Tochukwu E. Ogri,Muzaffar Qureshi,Kyle Volle,Rushikesh Kamalapurkar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于稀疏促进积分并发学习（SP-ICL）的自适应控制律，用于线性参数化的不确定非线性仿射系统，通过ℓ₁正则化实现参数稀疏性，并采用非光滑Lyapunov分析证明闭环系统轨迹的最终有界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对线性参数化不确定非线性仿射系统，现有方法在在线参数学习时缺乏稀疏性促进机制。虽然ℓ₁正则化在系统辨识和机器学习中常用于稀疏性促进，但现有方法未能将其与积分并发学习（ICL）在线更新律有效结合。

Method: 开发了SP-ICL自适应律，将ℓ₁正则化惩罚项通过滑模控制技术与积分并发学习相结合。该方法在在线参数更新过程中集成稀疏性促进机制，采用非光滑Lyapunov分析理论进行稳定性证明。

Result: 通过非光滑Lyapunov分析证明了闭环系统轨迹的最终有界性。仿真验证了SP-ICL更新律中稀疏性惩罚在轨迹跟踪过程中有效恢复稀疏动态特性的能力。

Conclusion: 提出的SP-ICL自适应律成功将ℓ₁正则化稀疏性促进机制集成到在线参数更新过程中，为不确定非线性系统的稀疏动态特性恢复提供了有效的控制框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a sparsity-promoting integral concurrent learning (SP-ICL) adaptation law for a linearly parametrized uncertain nonlinear control-affine system. The unknown parameters are learned using ICL with sparsity-promoting $\ell_1$ regularization. The use of $\ell_1$ regularization for sparsity promotion is common in system identification and machine learning; however, unlike existing approaches, this paper develops an online parameter update law that integrates the regularization penalty with ICL via sliding modes. Using the SP-ICL update law, we show via non-smooth Lyapunov analysis that the trajectories of the closed-loop system are ultimately bounded. Simulations verify the effectiveness of the sparsity penalty in the SP-ICL update law on recovering sparse dynamics during trajectory tracking.

</details>


### [94] [Almost Sure Convergence of Riemannian Stochastic Gradient Descents: Varying Batch Sizes And Nonstandard Batch Forming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06350)
*Hao Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在底层概率空间随迭代变化的Riemannian随机梯度下降算法的几乎必然收敛性，并将其应用于具有变化批量大小和无偏批量形成方案的Riemannian随机梯度下降


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Riemannian流形上的随机优化问题时，实际应用中经常遇到概率空间随迭代变化的情况（如批量大小变化、批量形成方案变化），需要建立相应的收敛性理论来支持这类算法的实际应用

Method: 建立Riemannian随机梯度下降在变化概率空间下的收敛性理论框架，将传统固定概率空间的收敛分析扩展到概率空间随迭代变化的情形，并应用该框架分析具有变化批量大小和无偏批量形成方案的算法

Result: 证明了在底层概率空间随迭代变化的Riemannian随机梯度下降算法的几乎必然收敛性，为具有变化批量大小和无偏批量形成方案的Riemannian随机梯度下降提供了收敛保证

Conclusion: 该研究扩展了Riemannian随机梯度下降的收敛性理论，使其能够处理概率空间随迭代变化的更一般情况，为实际应用中常见的批量大小变化等场景提供了理论支持

Abstract: We establish almost sure convergence for Riemannian stochastic gradient descents in which the underlying probability spaces vary from iteration to iteration. As applications, we deduce almost sure convergence for Riemannian stochastic gradient descents with varying batch sizes and unbiased batch forming schemes.

</details>


### [95] [The Chambolle-Pock method also converges weakly with $0 < θ\le 1$ and $τσ\|L\|^{2} < 4θ(2-θ)/(1 - 2θ+ 9θ^{2} - 4θ^{3})$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06423)
*Manu Upadhyaya*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了Chambolle-Pock方法在实数希尔伯特空间中的收敛性，扩展了弱收敛理论到θ∈(0,1/2]的未探索区域，并建立了基于李雅普诺夫函数的统一收敛分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chambolle-Pock方法是解决凸凹鞍点问题和复合凸优化问题的标准一阶算法，但其收敛理论在松弛参数θ∈(0,1/2]区域尚未被充分研究。本文旨在填补这一理论空白，建立统一的收敛分析框架。

Method: 采用李雅普诺夫函数方法，在实数希尔伯特空间中分析Chambolle-Pock算法的收敛性。研究步长τ,σ>0和松弛参数0<θ≤1的条件，特别是当τσ|L|² ≤ 4θ(2-θ)/(1-2θ+9θ²-4θ³)时的收敛行为。

Result: 证明了在上述步长条件下，遍历对偶间隙以O(1/k)速率收敛；当不等式严格成立时，原始-对偶迭代弱收敛到KKT点。特别地，将弱收敛理论扩展到0<θ≤1/2的未探索区域。

Conclusion: 该研究为Chambolle-Pock方法提供了统一的收敛分析框架，扩展了理论适用范围，特别是填补了θ∈(0,1/2]区域的收敛理论空白，为算法参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: The Chambolle-Pock method, also known as the primal-dual hybrid gradient method, is a standard first-order algorithm for convex-concave saddle-point problems and composite convex optimization involving two proper, lower semicontinuous, convex functions and a bounded linear operator $L$. We study its convergence in real Hilbert spaces for step sizes $τ,σ>0$ and relaxation parameter $0<θ\le 1$. We prove that, if $τσ|L|^{2} \leq 4θ(2-θ)/(1 - 2θ+ 9θ^{2} - 4θ^{3})$, then the ergodic duality gap converges at rate $O(1/k)$, and that, when the inequality is strict, the primal-dual iterates converge weakly to a KKT point. In particular, this extends the weak-convergence theory to the previously unexplored regime $0<θ\le 1/2$. The proof is based on a Lyapunov function that remains uniformly valid over the entire interval $0<θ\le 1$.

</details>


### [96] [Feedback control of Lagrange multipliers for non-smooth constrained optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06511)
*V. Cerone,S. M. Fosson,S. Pirrera,A. Re,D. Regruto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于控制理论的复合目标函数约束优化框架，通过近端增广拉格朗日构造系统，设计静态和动态控制器得到两种新算法，在强凸假设下证明全局指数收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对包含不可微项的复合目标函数约束优化问题，需要开发新的理论框架和算法。现有方法在收敛性和理论分析方面存在局限，控制理论为这类问题提供了新的视角和工具。

Method: 基于近端增广拉格朗日公式构造系统，使平衡点对应优化问题的驻点。提出两种控制策略：静态控制器和动态控制器，分别对应两种新的优化算法。在强凸假设下进行理论分析。

Result: 建立了全局指数收敛的理论保证。通过数值实验验证了所提方法的有效性，与最先进方法相比表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 成功开发了基于控制理论的复合优化框架，提出的两种算法具有理论保证和实际性能优势，为约束优化问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, we develop a control-theoretic framework for constrained optimization problems with composite objective functions including non-differentiable terms. Building on the proximal augmented Lagrangian formulation, we construct a plant whose equilibria correspond to the stationary points of the optimization problem. Within this framework, we propose two control strategies - a static controller and a dynamic controller - leading to two novel optimization algorithms. We provide a theoretical analysis, establishing global exponential convergence under strong convexity assumptions. Finally, we demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods through numerical experiments, benchmarking their performance against state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [97] [Stochastic Auto-conditioned Fast Gradient Methods with Optimal Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06525)
*Yao Ji,Guanghui Lan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了随机自适应条件快速梯度法（stochastic AC-FGM），解决了随机复合凸优化中无需先验问题参数知识即可达到最优加速率的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机复合凸优化中，在不了解问题参数（如Lipschitz常数、迭代时域、噪声水平）的情况下实现最优加速率是一个核心挑战。现有参数无关随机方法要么无法达到加速率，要么依赖限制性假设（如有界域、有界梯度、已知迭代时域或严格次高斯噪声）。

Method: 提出了随机自适应条件快速梯度法（stochastic AC-FGM），这是确定性自动条件快速梯度法的随机变体。该方法完全自适应于Lipschitz常数、迭代时域和噪声水平，无需线搜索程序即可实现自适应步长选择和自适应小批量大小调整。

Result: 在标准有界条件方差假设下，随机AC-FGM实现了最优迭代复杂度O(1/√ε)和最优样本复杂度O(1/ε²)。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将自适应加速方法扩展到随机设置，解决了参数无关随机优化的关键挑战，实现了无需先验知识的最优收敛速率。

Abstract: Achieving optimal rates for stochastic composite convex optimization without prior knowledge of problem parameters remains a central challenge. In the deterministic setting, the auto-conditioned fast gradient method has recently been proposed to attain optimal accelerated rates without line-search procedures or prior knowledge of the Lipschitz smoothness constant, providing a natural prototype for parameter-free acceleration. However, extending this approach to the stochastic setting has proven technically challenging and remains open. Existing parameter-free stochastic methods either fail to achieve accelerated rates or rely on restrictive assumptions, such as bounded domains, bounded gradients, prior knowledge of the iteration horizon, or strictly sub-Gaussian noise. To address these limitations, we propose a stochastic variant of the auto-conditioned fast gradient method, referred to as stochastic AC-FGM. The proposed method is fully adaptive to the Lipschitz constant, the iteration horizon, and the noise level, enabling both adaptive stepsize selection and adaptive mini-batch sizing without line-search procedures. Under standard bounded conditional variance assumptions, we show that stochastic AC-FGM achieves the optimal iteration complexity of $O(1/\sqrt{\varepsilon})$ and the optimal sample complexity of $O(1/\varepsilon^2)$.

</details>


### [98] [A Generalized Sinkhorn Algorithm for Mean-Field Schrödinger Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06531)
*Asmaa Eldesoukey,Yongxin Chen,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种求解平均场薛定谔桥问题的Sinkhorn型递归算法，通过推广Hopf-Cole变换处理非局部相互作用带来的非凸性挑战


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平均场薛定谔桥问题在指导具有非局部相互作用的扩散过程到达目标分布方面具有重要应用，但非局部相互作用导致问题非凸，计算困难，需要新的求解方法

Method: 推广Hopf-Cole变换到平均场薛定谔桥问题，并基于此设计Sinkhorn型递归算法来求解相关的积分-偏微分方程组

Result: 在温和的相互作用势能假设下，证明了所提算法的收敛性保证，并通过具有排斥和吸引相互作用的数值算例验证了理论贡献

Conclusion: 提出的算法为求解具有非局部相互作用的平均场薛定谔桥问题提供了有效的计算框架，能够处理标准薛定谔桥方法难以解决的多智能体系统控制问题

Abstract: The mean-field Schrödinger bridge (MFSB) problem concerns designing a minimum-effort controller that guides a diffusion process with nonlocal interaction to reach a given distribution from another by a fixed deadline. Unlike the standard Schrödinger bridge, the dynamical constraint for MFSB is the mean-field limit of a population of interacting agents with controls. It serves as a natural model for large-scale multi-agent systems. The MFSB is computationally challenging because the nonlocal interaction makes the problem nonconvex. We propose a generalization of the Hopf-Cole transform for MFSB and, building on it, design a Sinkhorn-type recursive algorithm to solve the associated system of integro-PDEs. Under mild assumptions on the interaction potential, we discuss convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm. We present numerical examples with repulsive and attractive interactions to illustrate the theoretical contributions.

</details>


### [99] [Hot Standby in Ammonia Synthesis Reshapes Market Equilibrium in Renewable P2A Systems: A Potential Game Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06593)
*Yangjun Zeng,Yiwei Qiu,Xiaocong Sun,Jie Zhu,Jiarong Li,Shi Chen,Buxiang Zhou,Kaigui Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在电力制氨系统中引入热备用运行模式如何改善可再生氨生产商的市场竞争力，通过建立势博弈模型和迭代最优响应方法求解ε近似均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可再生氨合成系统由于运行灵活性有限，在电力与氢市场的纳什均衡中处于劣势地位。虽然氨合成反应器的热备用运行模式能提高灵活性，但引入了整数决策变量，使市场均衡分析变得复杂。

Method: 开发了势博弈模型，采用迭代最优响应方法推导出收敛的ε近似均衡，以解决包含整数决策变量的市场均衡分析难题。

Result: 案例研究表明，热备用运行使可再生氨生产商减少了对氢采购的依赖，利润提高了20.14%。更重要的是，热备用将市场均衡推向更互利的结果。

Conclusion: 热备用运行模式显著提升了可再生氨生产商在电力制氨系统中的市场竞争力，通过改善运行灵活性实现了更有利的市场均衡状态。

Abstract: Integrating renewable generation, hydrogen production, and renewable ammonia (RA) synthesis into power-to-ammonia (P2A) systems creates interactions across electricity and hydrogen markets. Limited operational flexibility, however, places RA at a disadvantage at the Nash equilibrium (NE). Recent advances in ammonia synthesis reactor design enable hot standby (HSB) operation, improving flexibility but introducing integer decision variables that complicate market equilibrium analysis. To address this challenge, we develop a potential game model and derive a convergent ε-approximate equilibrium via an iterative best-response approach. Case studies show that HSB reduces RA's reliance on hydrogen purchases and increases its profit by 20.14%. More importantly, HSB shifts the market equilibrium toward a more mutually beneficial outcome.

</details>


### [100] [Nesterov Flow May Travel Infinitely Long to Converge to a Minimizer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06651)
*Ernest K. Ryu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文证明Nesterov ODE在二维空间中收敛到极小值点时，轨道长度可以是无限的，即不满足可求长性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证明Nesterov ODE（Nesterov加速梯度法的连续时间模型）在凸势函数下会点收敛到极小值点。自然的问题是这种点收敛能否升级为可求长性，即收敛轨道是否具有有限路径长度。

Method: 通过在二维空间构造一个可微凸势函数，证明其流收敛到极小值点但仍累积无限路径长度。所有证明完全由OpenAI内部模型完成。

Result: 证明Nesterov ODE的点收敛不能保证可求长性，存在二维可微凸势函数使得轨道收敛到极小值点但路径长度为无限。

Conclusion: Nesterov ODE的收敛轨道不一定可求长，点收敛不蕴含有限路径长度，这揭示了连续时间加速梯度方法收敛性质的局限性。

Abstract: Recent work has established that the trajectory of the Nesterov ODE, a the continuous-time model of Nesterov's accelerated gradient method, exhibits point convergence towards a minimizer of a convex potential. A natural next question is whether this point convergence can be upgraded to rectifiability, namely whether the convergent orbit has finite path length. This work provides the answer in the negative by constructing a differentiable convex potential in $\mathbb{R}^2$ for which the flow converges to its minimizer but still accumulates infinite path length. All proofs of this work are due entirely to an internal model at OpenAI.

</details>


### [101] [Uncertainty Propagation in Stochastic Hybrid Systems with Dimension-Varying Resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06708)
*Tejaswi K. C.,Taeyoung Lee*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了具有维度变化重置映射的随机混合系统的概率密度演化，提出了基于概率通量推送的弱形式框架，统一处理重置导致的维度增减情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Frobenius-Perron公式通过边界条件表示重置诱导的概率转移，当重置将守卫集映射到另一模式的内部或低维子集时，这种方法不够充分。需要开发能统一描述此类系统的框架。

Method: 开发弱形式公式，其中重置诱导的转移通过守卫集上概率通量的推送来表示。当重置降低维度时，转移概率表现为内部源密度；当重置增加维度时，通常表现为低维子集上的奇异源。

Result: 提出的框架自然地捕捉了两种情况：维度减少时转移概率作为内部源密度出现，维度增加时作为低维子集上的奇异源出现。通过两个粒子合并再分裂的随机混合模型进行了说明。

Conclusion: 维度变化的重置导致超出经典边界条件公式的源项，所提出的基于概率通量推送的弱形式框架为这类随机混合系统提供了统一描述。

Abstract: This paper studies probability density evolution for stochastic hybrid systems with reset maps that change the dimension of the continuous state across modes. Existing Frobenius--Perron formulations typically represent reset-induced probability transfer through boundary conditions, which is insufficient when resets map guard sets into the interior or onto lower-dimensional subsets of another mode. We develop a weak-form formulation in which reset-induced transfer is represented by the pushforward of probability flux across the guard, yielding a unified description for such systems. The proposed framework naturally captures both cases: when the reset decreases dimension, the transferred probability appears as an interior source density, whereas when the reset increases dimension, it generally appears as a singular source supported on a lower-dimensional subset. The approach is illustrated using a stochastic hybrid model in which two particles merge into one and later split back into two, demonstrating how dimension-changing resets lead to source terms beyond classical boundary-condition-based formulations.

</details>


### [102] [Linear Programming Problem Solved By a Special Substitution Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06726)
*Laurent Truffet*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种特殊的替代方法求解线性规划问题，该方法基于特定变量选择标准，具有强多项式复杂度，且无需矩阵求逆即可找到最优解对应的顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种不同于傅里叶-莫茨金消元法的特殊替代方法来解决线性规划问题，该方法通过特定的变量选择标准实现高效求解。

Method: 基于成本函数相关标准选择变量进行替代，证明标准搜索具有强多项式复杂度，该方法继承了经典替代方法的强多项式特性，且逆向替代寻找最优顶点时无需矩阵求逆。

Result: 该方法具有强多项式复杂度，能够高效求解线性规划问题，且逆向替代过程保持有效，无需进行矩阵求逆操作。

Conclusion: 提出的特殊替代方法为线性规划问题提供了一种强多项式复杂度的求解方案，既保持了经典替代方法的优势，又避免了矩阵求逆的计算负担。

Abstract: In this paper we develop a very special substitution method for solving
  a general linear programming problem (LPP). Of course the substitution is a kind of elimination
  of variable but this method must not be confused
  with the so-called Fourier-Motzkin elimination. The susbtitution developed in this
  paper only differs by the set of criteria that a variable must verify to be
  substitued. Most of the criteria are associated with the cost function of the
  LPP. We prove that the research of the criteria is strongly polynomial.
  Thus, the special substitution inehrits of the strong polynomiality which characterizes
  the classical substitution for linear systems. Moreover, as for the classical substitution the
  backward substitution for finding a vertex associated with the optimum is still valid and
  does not require to inverse a matrix.

</details>


### [103] [Mini-Batch Stochastic Krasnosel'ski\uı-Mann Algorithm for Nonexpansive Fixed Point Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06909)
*Hideaki Iiduka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种基于可计算小批量随机映射的Krasnosel'skiı-Mann算法变体，用于解决大规模非扩张映射固定点计算问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Krasnosel'skiı-Mann算法虽然理论保证强，但无法应用于某些实际大规模问题，主要障碍是映射计算困难

Method: 定义可计算的小批量随机映射作为非扩张映射的无偏估计器，将其集成到Krasnosel'skiı-Mann算法中，并采用递增批量大小策略

Result: 证明算法在批量大小递增条件下几乎必然收敛到非扩张映射的固定点，并进行了收敛速率分析

Conclusion: 通过引入随机小批量技术，成功扩展了Krasnosel'skiı-Mann算法在大规模问题中的应用范围，同时保持了收敛性保证

Abstract: The Krasnosel'ski\uı-Mann algorithm is a well-known method for finding fixed points of a nonexpansive mapping with strong theoretical guarantees. However, there are practical large-scale problems to which this algorithm cannot be applied. Here, to resolve the issue caused by the computational difficulty of the mapping, we define a computable mini-batch stochastic mapping, which is a unbiased estimator of the nonexpansive mapping, and implement it in the Krasnosel'ski\uı-Mann algorithm. We show that the algorithm with increasing batch sizes converges almost surely to a fixed point of the nonexpansive mapping. We also perform a convergence rate analysis on the algorithm.

</details>


### [104] [Multi-layer Predictor Feedback Design for Nonlinear Integro-Differential Equations with State-dependent Input Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06918)
*Tong Li,Peipei Shang,Mamadou Diagne*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的多层预测器反馈方法，用于精确补偿一般非线性积分微分方程的状态相关输入时滞，解决了传统PDE反步法预测器反馈设计中尚未处理的两个特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对具有状态相关输入时滞的非线性系统，特别是那些具有非常规混合PDE-ODE结构的系统，其中非线性ODE通过非齐次对流PDE驱动，且PDE的传播速度取决于ODE状态的移动窗口积分。这两个特征在标准PDE反步法预测器反馈设计中尚未得到解决。

Method: 开发多层预测器反馈控制设计，采用特征方法和不动点论证进行稳定性和适定性分析，而非传统的Lyapunov方法。系统由非线性ODE和非齐次对流PDE组成，PDE传播速度由ODE状态的积分决定。

Result: 在PDE控制方程中非线性项一致Lipschitz连续的温和假设下，两种设计均实现了PDE和ODE状态在supremum范数下的全局渐近稳定性。数值模拟验证了该方法在缓冲调节生产线和排队系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制设计能够处理状态相关时滞和非线性积分微分方程，为缓冲调节生产系统和排队系统提供了有效的控制解决方案，在局部安全软化的bang-bang反馈律下保持系统状态和执行信号的正性。

Abstract: We develop a novel multi-layer predictor-feedback to achieve exact compensation of state-dependent input delay of general nonlinear integro-differential equations. The system of interest is an unconventional mixed Partial Differential Equation (PDE)-Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) system, in which a nonlinear ODE is actuated through an inhomogeneous advection PDE. Moreover, the propagation speed of the PDE depends on a moving window integral of the ODE state. The two above features are not addressed yet in standard PDE backstepping-based predictor-feedback designs. Unlike the conventional Lyapunov-based approaches used in the field, our stability and well-posedness analysis rely on the characteristic method and a fixed-point argument. Both of our designs achieve global asymptotic stability (GAS) in the supremum norm of the PDE and ODE states under the mild assumption that the nonlinearity in the PDE governing equation is uniformly Lipschitz continuous. The transport speed, governed by the integral of the ODE state, models systems such as production or queuing processes in which the state of a finite buffer-namely, the inventory level-determines the production or service rate. Numerical simulations demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed control design for buffer-regulated production lines and queuing systems, ensuring asymptotic stability under a locally safe softened bang-bang feedback law that preserves the positivity of both the system state and the actuation signal.

</details>


### [105] [Continuous-Time Dynamics of the Difference-of-Convex Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06926)
*Yi-Shuai Niu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文研究了光滑DC分解下凸差算法(DCA)的连续时间结构，揭示了DCA与非线性自治系统的显式欧拉离散化之间的等价关系，提出了阻尼DCA方案并分析了其收敛性，建立了连续时间极限流与Bregman几何的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究DCA的连续时间结构，从动力系统视角理解DCA的收敛行为，通过建立离散算法与连续流之间的联系来获得更深入的收敛性分析，并为DC分解的质量提供几何判据。

Method: 1. 在对偶坐标下，将经典DCA解释为非线性自治系统的全步长显式欧拉离散化；2. 提出阻尼DCA方案（也是Bregman正则化DCA变体）；3. 分析阻尼DCA的单调下降、渐近临界性、KL收敛性；4. 研究步长趋于零时的极限流（Hessian-Riemannian梯度流）；5. 建立全局-局部权衡分析框架。

Result: 1. 证明了阻尼DCA的单调下降、渐近临界性、有界性下的KL收敛性，以及在度量DC-PL不等式下的全局线性收敛率；2. 对于极限流，建立了精确能量恒等式、有界轨迹的渐近临界性、度量相对误差界下的显式全局收敛率、KL假设下的有限长度和单点收敛性，以及非退化局部极小值附近的局部指数收敛性；3. 揭示了全局-局部权衡：半松弛方案在框架内具有最佳可证明全局保证，而全步长方案在非退化极小值附近局部最快；4. 展示了不同DC分解通过凸分量生成的度量诱导不同的连续动力学，为分解质量提供了几何判据。

Conclusion: 论文通过连续时间视角为DCA提供了统一的收敛性分析框架，揭示了DCA与Bregman几何的深层联系，为DC分解的质量评估提供了几何判据，并阐明了不同DCA变体在全局收敛保证与局部收敛速度之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: We study the continuous-time structure of the difference-of-convex algorithm (DCA) for smooth DC decompositions with a strongly convex component. In dual coordinates, classical DCA is exactly the full-step explicit Euler discretization of a nonlinear autonomous system. This viewpoint motivates a damped DCA scheme, which is also a Bregman-regularized DCA variant, and whose vanishing-step limit yields a Hessian-Riemannian gradient flow generated by the convex part of the decomposition. For the damped scheme we prove monotone descent, asymptotic criticality, Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz convergence under boundedness, and a global linear rate under a metric DC-PL inequality. For the limiting flow we establish an exact energy identity, asymptotic criticality of bounded trajectories, explicit global rates under metric relative error bounds, finite-length and single-point convergence under a Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz hypothesis, and local exponential convergence near nondegenerate local minima. The analysis also reveals a global-local tradeoff: the half-relaxed scheme gives the best provable global guarantee in our framework, while the full-step scheme is locally fastest near a nondegenerate minimum. Finally, we show that different DC decompositions of the same objective induce different continuous dynamics through the metric generated by the convex component, providing a geometric criterion for decomposition quality and linking DCA with Bregman geometry.

</details>


### [106] [Adaptive Distributionally Robust Optimal Control with Bayesian Ambiguity Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06936)
*Wentao Ma,Zhiping Chen,Huifu Xu,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应分布鲁棒最优控制模型，通过贝叶斯学习更新模糊集，解决数据稀缺和样本生成具有阶段性特征时的保守性问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布鲁棒最优控制模型多为离线方法，在数据稀缺时过于保守，且无法处理阶段性生成的样本。受Shapiro等人提出的贝叶斯SOC框架启发，需要开发自适应模型来应对这些挑战

Method: 提出基于贝叶斯学习的自适应DROC模型，模糊集随新数据更新；推导易处理的风险规避重构；建立无限时域SOC的最优值函数和策略一致性；提出Bellman算子割平面算法求解

Result: 获得有限样本后验可信度保证；证明模型对样本扰动的稳定性和统计鲁棒性；BOCP算法计算高效且收敛性可证；库存控制数值实验验证模型和算法的有效性、自适应性和鲁棒性能

Conclusion: 提出的自适应贝叶斯DROC模型能够有效处理数据稀缺和阶段性样本问题，在保持鲁棒性的同时减少保守性，为实际应用提供理论保证和高效算法

Abstract: In stochastic optimal control (SOC), uncertainty may arise from incomplete knowledge of the true probability distribution of the underlying environment, which is known as Knightian or epistemic uncertainty. Distributionally robust optimal control (DROC) models are subsequently proposed to tackle this source of uncertainty. While such models are effective in some practical applications, most existing DROC models are offline and can be overly conservative when data are scarce. Moreover, they cannot be applied to the case when samples are generated episodically. Motivated by the Bayesian SOC framework recently proposed by Shapiro et al.~\cite{shapiro2025episodic}, we propose an adaptive DROC model in which the ambiguity set is updated via Bayesian learning from new data. Under some moderate conditions, we derive a tractable risk-averse reformulation, establish consistency of the optimal value function and optimal policy for an infinite-horizon SOC and establish a finite-sample posterior credibility guarantee for the policy value induced by the proposed episodic Bayesian DROC model. We also study the stability and statistical robustness of the proposed model with respect to sample perturbations that often arise in data-driven environments. To solve the episodic Bayesian DROC model, we propose a Bellman-operator cutting-plane (BOCP) algorithm that is computationally efficient and provably convergent. Numerical results on an inventory control problem demonstrate the effectiveness, adaptivity, and robust performance of the proposed model and algorithm.

</details>


### [107] [Model-Free Aggregative Cooperative Optimization via Randomized Gradient-Free Minimization and Exploration Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07164)
*Amir Mehrnoosh,Giuseppe Speciale,Riccardo Brumali,Giuseppe Notarstefano,Gianluca Bianchin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出ARGFree算法，一种用于解决聚合合作优化问题的随机无梯度方法，无需梯度信息即可收敛到近似最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式决策场景中，每个智能体的目标函数既依赖于自身决策，也依赖于反映全局系统行为的聚合变量。实际应用中梯度信息往往不可得，因此需要开发无需梯度信息的算法来解决这类聚合合作优化问题。

Method: 提出ARGFree算法，结合有限差分梯度近似和跟踪变量，模拟基于梯度的方法行为。进一步提出改进版本ARGFree-EM，在探索信号中引入动量机制，平滑梯度探索信号的突变，提高分布式跟踪机制的准确性。

Result: 证明了ARGFree算法在期望意义下收敛到近似最优解，近似误差源于随机梯度估计器的使用。ARGFree-EM在高维设置中表现更优。

Conclusion: ARGFree方法是文献中首个能够无需梯度信息解决聚合合作优化问题的算法类别，为实际分布式决策问题提供了有效的无梯度解决方案。

Abstract: Aggregative cooperative optimization problems arise in distributed decision-making settings where each agent's objective depends on its own decision as well as on an aggregate variable capturing global system behavior. Motivated by practical scenarios where gradient information is unavailable, this paper introduces a randomized gradient-free algorithm, named ARGFree, for solving such problems. ARGFree combines finite-difference gradient approximations with a set of tracking variables, emulating the behavior of a gradient-based method. We prove that ARGFree converges in expectation to an approximate optimizer, with the approximation error stemming from the use of a randomized gradient estimator. To enhance performance in high-dimensional settings, we further propose an improved variant, ARGFree-EM, which incorporates momentum in the exploration signals to smooth sudden fluctuations in the gradient exploration signals and thereby improve the accuracy of the underlying distributed tracking mechanism. To the best of our knowledge, the class of ARGFree methods is the first in the literature capable of solving aggregating cooperative optimization problems without gradient information.

</details>


### [108] [An Inexact Trust-Region Method for Structured Nonsmooth Optimization with Application to Risk-Averse Stochastic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07216)
*Drew P. Kouri*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种信赖域方法，用于高效最小化光滑函数、非光滑凸函数与有限值支撑函数复合的求和问题，支持不精确值和导数信息，适用于PDE约束优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有该结构的优化问题在风险规避随机规划和用于非光滑惩罚非线性规划方法的子问题等众多应用中广泛出现。需要处理PDE约束的无限维问题，允许使用不精确的值和导数信息

Method: 开发一种信赖域方法，用于最小化光滑函数、非光滑凸函数与有限值支撑函数复合的求和。方法允许使用不精确的值和导数信息，能够处理由偏微分方程控制的无限维问题

Result: 证明了方法的全局收敛性，在额外正则性假设下，证明了迭代序列在目标问题的驻点处累积。在两个PDE约束优化示例中展示了方法的高效性，性能不随PDE离散化规模变化

Conclusion: 该方法能够高效处理具有特定结构的优化问题，特别适用于PDE约束优化，性能对离散化规模不敏感，具有理论和实际应用价值

Abstract: We develop a trust-region method for efficiently minimizing the sum of a smooth function, a nonsmooth convex function, and the composition of a finite-valued support function with a smooth function. Optimization problems with this structure arise in numerous applications including risk-averse stochastic programming and subproblems for nonsmooth penalty nonlinear programming methods. Our method permits the use of inexact value and derivative information, enabling the solution of infinite-dimensional problems governed by, e.g., partial differential equations (PDEs). We prove global convergence of our method and under additional regularity assumptions, demonstrate that the sequence of iterates accumulates at a stationary point of our target problem. We demonstrate our method's efficiency on two PDE-constrained optimization examples, showing that its performance is invariant to the PDE discretization size.

</details>


### [109] [A Trajectory-based Approach to the Computation of Controlled Invariants with application to MPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07225)
*Emmanuel Junior Wafo Wembe,Adnane Saoud*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出基于轨迹视角的线性离散时间系统控制不变集计算方法，引入凸可行点概念，结合经典后向不动点算法计算最大控制不变集，并设计无需预计算终端集的MPC方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制不变集计算方法存在局限性，需要新的视角来更有效地计算控制不变集，特别是为模型预测控制（MPC）提供递归可行性保证而不依赖预计算的终端集。

Method: 1. 引入凸可行点概念，利用有限长状态轨迹表征控制不变性；2. 结合经典后向不动点算法计算最大控制不变集；3. 提出两种无需预计算终端集的MPC方案；4. 将凸可行点搜索转化为优化问题，提供实际计算方法。

Result: 通过数值示例验证了方法的有效性，能够成功计算控制不变集，并实现MPC的递归可行性保证。

Conclusion: 基于轨迹视角的控制不变集计算方法为线性离散时间系统提供了新的理论框架和实用工具，特别适用于MPC设计，无需依赖预计算的终端集即可保证递归可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we revisit the computation of controlled invariant sets for linear discrete-time systems through a trajectory-based viewpoint. We begin by introducing the notion of convex feasible points, which provides a new characterization of controlled invariance using finitely long state trajectories. We further show that combining this notion with the classical backward fixed-point algorithm allows us to compute the maximal controlled invariant set. Building on these results, we propose two MPC schemes that guarantee recursive feasibility without relying on precomputed terminal sets. Finally, we formulate the search for convex feasible points as an optimization problem, yielding a practical computational method for constructing controlled invariant sets. The effectiveness of the approach is illustrated through numerical examples.

</details>


### [110] [Non-Lipschitz Inertial Contraction-Type Method for Monotone Variational Inclusion problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07241)
*Feeroz Babu,Syed Shakaib Irfan,Jen-Chih Yao,Xiaopeng Zhao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种惯性收缩型方法求解单调变分包含问题，无需单值算子的Lipschitz连续性和强制性条件，获得弱收敛和强收敛结果


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有收缩型方法通常要求单值算子具有Lipschitz连续性和单调性或共强制性，限制了应用范围。本文旨在开发一种不依赖这些严格条件的算法

Method: 提出惯性收缩型方法，利用惯性技术加速收敛。算法不要求单值算子的Lipschitz连续性和强制性条件，仅需单调性。对于集值算子，利用其极大单调性和强单调性建立强收敛

Result: 获得弱收敛结果，收敛速度为$\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{k})$。当集值算子具有极大单调性和强单调性时，获得线性收敛率的强收敛结果。数值实验验证了算法在信号恢复问题中的有效性

Conclusion: 提出的惯性收缩型方法成功解决了传统方法对Lipschitz连续性和强制性条件的依赖问题，为单调变分包含问题提供了更通用的求解框架，并在理论和数值上验证了其有效性

Abstract: This study explores an inertial-based contraction-type approach for addressing monotone variational inclusion problems (in short, MVIP) within real Hilbert spaces. Most contraction-type techniques assume Lipschitz continuity and monotonicity or co-coercivity (inverse strongly monotone) of the single-valued operator. However, the key advantage of the proposed method is that it does not rely on the coercivity condition and the Lipschitz continuity for the single-valued operator. A weak convergence result has been achieved for the proposed algorithm with a convergence rate $\mathcal{O}\left(1/\sqrt{k}\right)$. In addition, the maximal and strong monotonicity of the set-valued operator is used to establish a strong convergence result with the linear convergence rate. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed method, we conduct numerical experiments focused on signal recovery problems.

</details>


### [111] [Negative curvature obstructs the existence of good barriers for interior-point methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07319)
*Christopher Criscitiello,Harold Nieuwboer,Michael Walter*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文证明在双曲空间等对称Hadamard流形中，自然区域（如测地球和三角形）的障碍参数随直径多项式增长，导致内点法无法高效解决具有指数大直径域的缩放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究内点法在Hadamard流形上推广的局限性，特别是障碍参数的增长如何影响算法效率，以理解为何内点法无法高效解决张量缩放等具有大直径域的挑战性问题。

Method: 通过分析双曲空间中自然区域（测地球、三角形等）的障碍参数，证明其随区域直径多项式增长，并将结果推广到正定矩阵和其他对称Hadamard空间。

Result: 在双曲空间等对称Hadamard流形中，自然区域的障碍参数随直径多项式增长，这导致基于球障碍的内点法无法高效解决具有指数大直径域的缩放问题。

Conclusion: 内点法在Hadamard流形上的扩展存在根本性限制：当问题域直径指数大时，基于障碍函数的内点法无法提供高效解决方案，这对张量缩放等问题的算法设计提出了挑战。

Abstract: Interior-point methods (IPMs) are a cornerstone of Euclidean convex optimization, due to their strong theoretical guarantees and practical performance. Motivated by scaling problems, recent work by Hirai and the last two authors (FOCS'23) extended IPMs to geodesically convex optimization on Hadamard manifolds. Crucially, the complexity of IPMs (both in Euclidean and Hadamard spaces) is governed by the \emph{barrier parameter} of the domain. Here we prove that already in hyperbolic space, several natural domains -- including geodesic balls and triangles -- have a barrier parameter that grows polynomially with the domain's diameter. By extension, the same holds for the positive-definite matrices and other symmetric Hadamard spaces. This growth implies a fundamental limitation: interior-point methods relying on barriers for a ball cannot efficiently solve challenging scaling problems, such as tensor scaling, where the domain's diameter can be exponentially large in the input size. Our results are partially inspired by, and complement, lower bounds on the condition number of geodesically convex functions established by Hamilton and Moitra (NeurIPS'21).

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [112] [FLeX: Fourier-based Low-rank EXpansion for multilingual transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06253)
*Gaurav Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过参数高效微调方法和优化器增强来改进从Python到Java等语言的跨语言代码生成，使用LoRA微调Code Llama 7B模型，比较Adam和Sophia优化器，并探索基于傅里叶的正则化技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业环境中多种编程语言共存，跨语言代码生成至关重要，但为每种语言单独微调大型语言模型计算成本过高。需要探索参数高效的方法来改进跨语言迁移。

Method: 使用低秩适应（LoRA）微调Code Llama 7B模型，仅优化一小部分参数；比较Adam和Sophia优化器；探索基于傅里叶的正则化技术；在小型高质量数据集MBPP上进行实验。

Result: 1) LoRA微调在MBPP数据集上的pass@1性能（40.1%）超过了更广泛微调的Code Llama-Python-7B模型（38.4%）；2) Sophia比Adam收敛更快，但最终pass@1分数差异不大；3) 傅里叶正则化显著改善跨语言迁移，在Java任务上达到42.1% pass@1，相比基线34.2%。

Conclusion: 结合LoRA、优化的训练方法和频域正则化，可以高效地使单语言LLM适应多种编程语言，为跨语言代码生成提供计算高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Cross-lingual code generation is critical in enterprise environments where multiple programming languages coexist. However, fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) individually for each language is computationally prohibitive. This paper investigates whether parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods and optimizer enhancements can improve cross-lingual transfer from Python to languages like Java. We fine-tune the Code Llama 7B model using low-rank adaptation (LoRA) to optimize a small subset of parameters and compare Adam and Sophia optimizers, while exploring a novel Fourier-based regularization technique. Our contributions include: (1)demonstrating that LoRA fine-tuning on a small, high-quality dataset (MBPP) can exceed the pass@1 performance of the more broadly fine-tuned Code Llama-Python-7B model (40.1% vs. 38.4%); (2) showing that while Sophia achieves faster convergence than Adam, final pass@1 scores show marginal differences; and (3) presenting evidence that Fourier-based regularization during fine-tuning significantly improves cross-lingual transfer, achieving 42.1% pass@1 on Java tasks compared to the 34.2% baseline. These findings suggest that combining LoRA, optimized training methods, and frequency-domain regularization can efficiently adapt single-language LLMs to perform well across multiple programming languages.

</details>


### [113] [Spectral Edge Dynamics Reveal Functional Modes of Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06256)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练动态在grokking过程中集中在少数主导更新方向（谱边缘），这些方向揭示了任务代数对称性决定的低维功能模式，而非参数或特征空间的局部结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究grokking现象中训练动态的本质特征，探索为什么传统的机制可解释性工具（如头归因、激活探测、稀疏自编码器）无法捕捉到grokking与非grokking机制之间的关键区别。

Method: 通过分析训练动态的谱边缘（主导更新方向），研究不同数学任务（模加法、乘法、减法、x²+y²）中这些方向在输入域上诱导的结构化函数，并与传统可解释性方法进行对比。

Result: 1. 谱边缘方向不是参数或特征空间的局部结构，而是输入域上的结构化函数；2. 模加法中所有主导方向坍缩为单一傅里叶模式；3. 乘法中仅在离散对数基下出现类似坍缩，浓度提高5.9倍；4. 减法中谱边缘跨越小型多模式族；5. x²+y²任务中无单一谐波基足够，但加性和乘性特征的交叉项提供4倍方差提升；6. 多任务训练增强组合结构，x²+y²谱边缘继承加法电路的特征频率（浓度提高2.3倍）。

Conclusion: 训练发现输入域上的低维功能模式，其结构取决于任务的代数对称性。简单谐波结构仅当任务允许对称适应基时出现；更复杂的任务需要更丰富的功能描述。谱边缘动态识别了控制学习的低维功能子空间，其表示取决于任务的代数结构。

Abstract: Training dynamics during grokking concentrate along a small number of dominant update directions -- the spectral edge -- which reliably distinguishes grokking from non-grokking regimes. We show that standard mechanistic interpretability tools (head attribution, activation probing, sparse autoencoders) fail to capture these directions: their structure is not localized in parameter or feature space. Instead, each direction induces a structured function over the input domain, revealing low-dimensional functional modes invisible to representation-level analysis.
  For modular addition, all leading directions collapse to a single Fourier mode. For multiplication, the same collapse appears only in the discrete-log basis, yielding a 5.9x improvement in concentration. For subtraction, the edge spans a small multi-mode family. For $x^2+y^2$, no single harmonic basis suffices, but cross-terms of additive and multiplicative features provide a 4x variance boost, consistent with the decomposition (a+b)^2 - 2ab. Multitask training amplifies this compositional structure, with the $x^2+y^2$ spectral edge inheriting the addition circuit's characteristic frequency (2.3x concentration increase).
  These results suggest that training discovers low-dimensional functional modes over the input domain, whose structure depends on the algebraic symmetry of the task.
  These results suggest that spectral edge dynamics identify low-dimensional functional subspaces governing learning, whose representation depends on the algebraic structure of the task. Simple harmonic structure emerges only when the task admits a symmetry-adapted basis; more complex tasks require richer functional descriptions.

</details>


### [114] [MO-RiskVAE: A Multi-Omics Variational Autoencoder for Survival Risk Modeling in Multiple MyelomaMO-RiskVAE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06267)
*Zixuan Chen,Heng Zhang,YuPeng Qin,WenPeng Xing,Qiang Wang,Da Wang,Changting Lin,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析了多模态VAE在生存风险建模中的潜在空间设计选择，发现生存监督训练主要对正则化强度和结构敏感而非特定散度公式，提出了改进的MO-RiskVAE模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态变分自编码器在多发性骨髓瘤生存风险建模中显示出潜力，但标准潜在正则化策略在生存监督训练下往往无法保留预后相关信息，导致表示不稳定或过度约束。尽管有多种变体提出，但潜在设计的哪些方面从根本上影响性能仍不清楚。

Method: 在统一的MyeVAE框架扩展中，系统隔离了正则化强度、后验几何和潜在空间结构等潜在建模选择。研究了KL正则化、MMD和HSIC等不同散度机制，并探索了基于Gumbel-Softmax的混合连续-离散潜在空间结构。

Result: 生存驱动训练主要对潜在正则化的强度和结构敏感，而非特定散度公式。适度放松KL正则化能持续改善生存区分能力，而MMD和HSIC等替代机制在没有适当缩放时益处有限。结构化潜在空间能改善学习表示与生存风险梯度之间的对齐。

Conclusion: 基于这些发现，提出了MO-RiskVAE模型，该模型在不引入额外监督或复杂训练启发式的情况下，持续改进了原始MyeVAE的风险分层能力。混合连续-离散公式增强了连续潜在子空间中的全局风险排序。

Abstract: Multimodal variational autoencoders (VAEs) have emerged as a powerful framework for survival risk modeling in multiple myeloma by integrating heterogeneous omics and clinical data. However, when trained under survival supervision, standard latent regularization strategies often fail to preserve prognostically relevant variation, leading to unstable or overly constrained representations. Despite numerous proposed variants, it remains unclear which aspects of latent design fundamentally govern performance in this setting. In this work, we conduct a controlled investigation of latent modeling choices for multimodal survival prediction within a unified extension of the MyeVAE framework. By systematically isolating regularization scale, posterior geometry, and latent space structure under identical architectures and optimization protocols, we show that survival-driven training is primarily sensitive to the magnitude and structure of latent regularization rather than the specific divergence formulation. In particular, moderate relaxation of KL regularization consistently improves survival discrimination, while alternative divergence mechanisms such as MMD and HSIC provide limited benefit without appropriate scaling. We further demonstrate that structuring the latent space can improve alignment between learned representations and survival risk gradients. A hybrid continuous--discrete formulation based on Gumbel--Softmax enhances global risk ordering in the continuous latent subspace, even though stable discrete subtype discovery does not emerge under survival supervision. Guided by these findings, we instantiate a robust multimodal survival model, termed MO-RiskVAE, which consistently improves risk stratification over the original MyeVAE without introducing additional supervision or complex training heuristics.

</details>


### [115] [TalkLoRA: Communication-Aware Mixture of Low-Rank Adaptation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06291)
*Lin Mu,Haiyang Wang,Li Ni,Lei Sang,Zhize Wu,Peiquan Jin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TalkLoRA 是一种通信感知的 MoE-LoRA 框架，通过专家间通信机制改进路由稳定性，在保持参数效率的同时提升语言模型微调性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于混合专家（MoE）的LoRA方法假设专家独立运作，导致路由不稳定和专家主导问题。需要打破专家独立性假设，通过专家间通信来改善路由决策。

Method: 提出TalkLoRA框架，在路由前引入专家级通信。为低秩专家配备轻量级Talking Module，实现跨专家子空间的信息交换，生成更鲁棒的全局信号用于路由。

Result: 在多种语言理解和生成任务中，TalkLoRA持续优于普通LoRA和MoELoRA，在相同参数预算下实现更高的参数效率和更平衡的专家路由。

Conclusion: 结构化专家通信是基于MoE的参数高效适应的原则性和有效增强方法，TalkLoRA通过专家间通信平滑路由动态，严格泛化现有MoELoRA架构。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs), and recent Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) extensions further enhance flexibility by dynamically combining multiple LoRA experts. However, existing MoE-augmented LoRA methods assume that experts operate independently, often leading to unstable routing, expert dominance. In this paper, we propose \textbf{TalkLoRA}, a communication-aware MoELoRA framework that relaxes this independence assumption by introducing expert-level communication prior to routing. TalkLoRA equips low-rank experts with a lightweight Talking Module that enables controlled information exchange across expert subspaces, producing a more robust global signal for routing. Theoretically, we show that expert communication smooths routing dynamics by mitigating perturbation amplification while strictly generalizing existing MoELoRA architectures. Empirically, TalkLoRA consistently outperforms vanilla LoRA and MoELoRA across diverse language understanding and generation tasks, achieving higher parameter efficiency and more balanced expert routing under comparable parameter budgets. These results highlight structured expert communication as a principled and effective enhancement for MoE-based parameter-efficient adaptation. Code is available at https://github.com/why0129/TalkLoRA.

</details>


### [116] [Drifting Fields are not Conservative](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06333)
*Leonard Franz,Sebastian Hoffmann,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 漂移模型通过向量值漂移场将生成样本向数据分布传输，但通常不是标量损失的梯度，只有高斯核是例外。作者提出通过锐化核的替代归一化方法恢复保守性，使漂移模型可用损失函数训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究漂移模型是否等价于优化标量损失，发现漂移场通常不是保守的（不能表示为标量势的梯度），这限制了使用损失函数训练漂移模型的可能性。

Method: 识别位置相关归一化是非保守性的来源，发现高斯核是唯一例外。提出使用锐化核进行替代归一化，为任何径向核恢复保守性，从而得到定义良好的损失函数用于训练漂移模型。

Result: 漂移场匹配目标比损失最小化更一般化，可以实现标量损失无法复制的非保守传输场，但实际增益有限。因此建议使用概念更简单的损失函数公式训练漂移模型。

Conclusion: 虽然漂移场匹配在理论上更灵活，但实践中使用损失函数训练漂移模型更为简单有效，通过锐化核归一化方法可以确保漂移场的保守性。

Abstract: Drifting models generate high-quality samples in a single forward pass by transporting generated samples toward the data distribution using a vector valued drift field. We investigate whether this procedure is equivalent to optimizing a scalar loss and find that, in general, it is not: drift fields are not conservative - they cannot be written as the gradient of any scalar potential. We identify the position-dependent normalization as the source of non-conservatism. The Gaussian kernel is the unique exception where the normalization is harmless and the drift field is exactly the gradient of a scalar function. Generalizing this, we propose an alternative normalization via a related kernel (the sharp kernel) which restores conservatism for any radial kernel, yielding well-defined loss functions for training drifting models. While we identify that the drifting field matching objective is strictly more general than loss minimization, as it can implement non-conservative transport fields that no scalar loss can reproduce, we observe that practical gains obtained utilizing this flexibility are minimal. We thus propose to train drifting models with the conceptually simpler formulations utilizing loss functions.

</details>


### [117] [BiScale-GTR: Fragment-Aware Graph Transformers for Multi-Scale Molecular Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06336)
*Yi Yang,Ovidiu Daescu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BiScale-GTR：结合化学片段分词与自适应多尺度推理的自监督分子表示学习框架，在多个基准测试中达到SOTA性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图Transformer方法大多仍以GNN为主导，导致表示过度受局部消息传递影响；同时大多数方法仅操作于单一结构粒度，难以捕捉跨多个分子尺度的模式

Method: 改进图字节对编码（BPE）分词以生成一致、化学有效且高覆盖率的片段token；采用并行GNN-Transformer架构，将GNN学习的原子级表示池化为片段级嵌入，与片段token嵌入融合后进行Transformer推理

Result: 在MoleculeNet、PharmaBench和LRGB基准测试中，在分类和回归任务上均达到最先进的性能；归因分析显示模型能突出化学上有意义的功能基团

Conclusion: BiScale-GTR通过结合化学片段分词与自适应多尺度推理，能够联合捕捉局部化学环境、子结构级基序和长程分子依赖关系，为分子结构-性质关系提供可解释性链接

Abstract: Graph Transformers have recently attracted attention for molecular property prediction by combining the inductive biases of graph neural networks (GNNs) with the global receptive field of Transformers. However, many existing hybrid architectures remain GNN-dominated, causing the resulting representations to remain heavily shaped by local message passing. Moreover, most existing methods operate at only a single structural granularity, limiting their ability to capture molecular patterns that span multiple molecular scales. We introduce BiScale-GTR, a unified framework for self-supervised molecular representation learning that combines chemically grounded fragment tokenization with adaptive multi-scale reasoning. Our method improves graph Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) tokenization to produce consistent, chemically valid, and high-coverage fragment tokens, which are used as fragment-level inputs to a parallel GNN-Transformer architecture. Architecturally, atom-level representations learned by a GNN are pooled into fragment-level embeddings and fused with fragment token embeddings before Transformer reasoning, enabling the model to jointly capture local chemical environments, substructure-level motifs, and long-range molecular dependencies. Experiments on MoleculeNet, PharmaBench, and the Long Range Graph Benchmark (LRGB) demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across both classification and regression tasks. Attribution analysis further shows that BiScale-GTR highlights chemically meaningful functional motifs, providing interpretable links between molecular structure and predicted properties. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [118] [The Master Key Hypothesis: Unlocking Cross-Model Capability Transfer via Linear Subspace Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06377)
*Rishab Balasubramanian,Pin-Jie Lin,Rituraj Sharma,Anjie Fang,Fardin Abdi,Viktor Rozgic,Zheng Du,Mohit Bansal,Tu Vu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UNLOCK框架无需训练即可在不同规模模型间迁移后训练能力，通过提取能力方向并在低维潜在子空间中进行线性对齐，显著提升推理性能。


<details>
